# Original building catalog

Generated by `tools/build_historical_catalog.py` from recovered bundle 4.0.1. All 274 imported definitions audited: 271 unique original objects and three synthetic compatibility definitions (10001, 10008, 10012). No duplicate original IDs found after manifest-file de-duplication. Same-name entries and land/color variants are not interchangeable IDs.

Evidence confidence: **high for literal recovered fields**, not a claim that every object was available in the live store. Missing fields are unknown/not supplied, not invented. `availableFromStore=0`, tutorial gates, upgrades, seasonal packs and server eligibility must be considered together. Unmapped art does not prove an object is unfinished in the original game.

Historical values below are unmodified: runtime uses compact footprints, accelerated construction and grandfathered early-game adapters. IDs 2/3/4 are civic upgrades; 12/37/142/14 are residential progression; 15/17/23/36 are processing/Pen; 32/38 are commercial adapters. Castle 5 → Citadel 221 (level 30) remains researched/planned until suitable complete art and implementation exist. Theater 167 is level 26 in this bundle, unlike the January 2011 level-28 recipe note; it is not currently playable. Jeweler 237 is level 41 and remains planned. These are version conflicts, not reasons to invent a combined table.

## Primary source map

Original [Objects manifest](../original/TradeNations.app/bundle/Objects.xml), [Settings](../original/TradeNations.app/bundle/Settings.xml), [Items](../original/TradeNations.app/bundle/Items.xml), [Resources](../original/TradeNations.app/bundle/Resources.xml). See [permanent references](ORIGINAL_GAME_REFERENCES.md) for version-scoped external evidence. Active `UpgradeOf` lists are retained in full; the older importer sometimes retained only their first entry.

## Research gaps and safety

Source rates are per worker per hour, not per-frame yields. Recipe prerequisites and currency units require explicit checks. The original MaxBuilding house/shop limits and 0–70 XP rows are preserved in the runtime evidence table; the playable cap remains 50. Historical land upgrades in Items.xml are not the same dimensions/prices as our existing compact-plot expansion. Adoption, expiry/event eligibility, buffs, original server orders and unconverted animation frames remain unresolved/unimplemented. Do not infer a feature is playable from this catalog. See PHASE_2_RESTORATION.md for tested scope and deviations.
